Offer Terms //*[@id='main-content']/div[2]/div[3]/p[1] Anytime Australia Pty Ltd (ABN 11 131 035 491), located at Level 2, 110 Alexander Street, Crows Nest NSW 2065, is the promoter of the Lift The Weight Challenge Buddy Pass – 8 Day Trial. The promotion runs from 12:00am AEST on 7 April 2026 and closes at 11:59pm AEST on 18 April 2026. It is open to individuals who have never been a member at any Anytime Fitness club (Guests) or whose previous membership ended on or before 1 January 2026 (Returning Guests), provided they are referred by a current, active Anytime Fitness member and attend the member’s home club during staffed hours, with that club participating in the promotion. Upon attending a participating club, Guests and Returning Guests will receive an eight (8) day free trial (the Trial Period), which begins on the date the Guest or Returning Guest completes their Anytime Fitness Guest Profile and ends at the expiry of the Trial Period.
